Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the crystals framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 471. The resulting operator achieves bound O(850/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the crystals space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 471 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 850. QED.
